Experiment: deepseek-chat_leadtime2_0_80_50_50_plain_no_no_15_default_m3_r9
Generation: 3
Mechanism: function_attributes
Train Objective: 5793.72183
Test Objective: 5713.17078

Description: I made several key improvements: 1) Increased base_stock and safety_stock to better handle the observed demand variability, 2) Added pipeline_weight for exponential weighting of recent orders to bette...

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    base_stock = 135  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 135, 'min': 100, 'max': 180, 'type': 'float'}
    safety_stock = 32  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 32, 'min': 15, 'max': 45, 'type': 'float'}
    demand_adj_factor = 1.1  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 1.1, 'min': 0.8, 'max': 1.4, 'type': 'float'}
    pipeline_weight = 0.6  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.6, 'min': 0.3, 'max': 0.9, 'type': 'float'}
    smoothing_factor = 0.7  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.7, 'min': 0.4, 'max': 0.9, 'type': 'float'}
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Calculate expected demand using weighted average of recent pipeline orders
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        # Give more weight to recent orders using exponential weighting
        weights = [pipeline_weight ** (len(pipeline_orders) - i - 1) for i in range(len(pipeline_orders))]
        total_weight = sum(weights)
        weighted_avg = sum(p * w for p, w in zip(pipeline_orders, weights)) / total_weight
        expected_demand = weighted_avg * demand_adj_factor
    else:
        expected_demand = 0
    
    # Dynamic base stock adjustment based on expected demand and safety stock
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock + safety_stock + expected_demand
    
    # Calculate raw order amount
    raw_order = max(0, adjusted_base_stock - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply exponential smoothing to prevent large order swings
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = smoothing_factor * compute_order_amount.prev_order + (1 - smoothing_factor) * raw_order
    else:
        smoothed_order = raw_order
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = smoothed_order
    
    # Cap order to prevent excessive ordering while maintaining responsiveness
    max_order = base_stock * 1.3
    order_amount = min(smoothed_order, max_order)
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    base_stock = 142  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 142, 'min': 120, 'max': 165, 'type': 'float'}
    safety_stock = 28  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 28, 'min': 20, 'max': 40, 'type': 'float'}
    demand_adj_factor = 1.05  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 1.05, 'min': 0.9, 'max': 1.2, 'type': 'float'}
    pipeline_weight = 0.75  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.75, 'min': 0.5, 'max': 0.95, 'type': 'float'}
    smoothing_factor = 0.8  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.8, 'min': 0.6, 'max': 0.95, 'type': 'float'}
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Calculate expected demand using weighted average of recent pipeline orders
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        # Give more weight to recent orders using exponential weighting
        weights = [pipeline_weight ** (len(pipeline_orders) - i - 1) for i in range(len(pipeline_orders))]
        total_weight = sum(weights)
        weighted_avg = sum(p * w for p, w in zip(pipeline_orders, weights)) / total_weight
        expected_demand = weighted_avg * demand_adj_factor
    else:
        expected_demand = 0
    
    # Dynamic base stock adjustment based on expected demand and safety stock
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock + safety_stock + expected_demand
    
    # Calculate raw order amount
    raw_order = max(0, adjusted_base_stock - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply exponential smoothing to prevent large order swings
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = smoothing_factor * compute_order_amount.prev_order + (1 - smoothing_factor) * raw_order
    else:
        smoothed_order = raw_order
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = smoothed_order
    
    # Cap order to prevent excessive ordering while maintaining responsiveness
    max_order = base_stock * 1.2
    order_amount = min(smoothed_order, max_order)
    
    return order_amount

Experiment: deepseek-chat_normal0_80_50_50_plain_no_no_15_default_m3_r12
Generation: 3
Mechanism: function_attributes
Train Objective: 3147.52047
Test Objective: 3098.63236

Description: I made several key improvements: 1) Increased base_stock to 145 to better handle the observed demand patterns, 2) Reduced safety_stock to 25 since the higher base_stock provides sufficient coverage, 3...

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    base_stock = 145.0  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 145.0, 'min': 100, 'max': 180, 'type': 'float'}
    safety_stock = 25.0  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 25.0, 'min': 15, 'max': 40, 'type': 'float'}
    demand_adj_factor = 1.05  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 1.05, 'min': 0.8, 'max': 1.2, 'type': 'float'}
    pipeline_weight = 0.3  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.3, 'min': 0.1, 'max': 0.6, 'type': 'float'}
    smoothing_factor = 0.7  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.7, 'min': 0.3, 'max': 0.9, 'type': 'float'}
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Estimate demand using recent pipeline orders with more robust calculation
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        recent_demand_estimate = sum(pipeline_orders) / len(pipeline_orders)
        # More conservative demand ratio with tighter bounds
        demand_ratio = min(1.2, max(0.8, recent_demand_estimate / 80))
        adjusted_base_stock = base_stock * (0.6 + pipeline_weight * demand_ratio)
    else:
        adjusted_base_stock = base_stock
    
    # Calculate target inventory level
    target_level = adjusted_base_stock + safety_stock
    
    # Calculate raw order amount
    raw_order = max(0, target_level * demand_adj_factor - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply exponential smoothing to order amount for stability
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = smoothing_factor * raw_order + (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.prev_order
        compute_order_amount.prev_order = smoothed_order
        order_amount = smoothed_order
    else:
        compute_order_amount.prev_order = raw_order
        order_amount = raw_order
    
    # Ensure integer order amount
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    base_stock = 145.0  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 145.0, 'min': 100, 'max': 180, 'type': 'float'}
    safety_stock = 25.0  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 25.0, 'min': 15, 'max': 40, 'type': 'float'}
    demand_adj_factor = 1.05  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 1.05, 'min': 0.8, 'max': 1.2, 'type': 'float'}
    pipeline_weight = 0.3  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.3, 'min': 0.1, 'max': 0.6, 'type': 'float'}
    smoothing_factor = 0.7  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.7, 'min': 0.3, 'max': 0.9, 'type': 'float'}
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Estimate demand using recent pipeline orders with more robust calculation
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        recent_demand_estimate = sum(pipeline_orders) / len(pipeline_orders)
        # More conservative demand ratio with tighter bounds
        demand_ratio = min(1.2, max(0.8, recent_demand_estimate / 80))
        adjusted_base_stock = base_stock * (0.6 + pipeline_weight * demand_ratio)
    else:
        adjusted_base_stock = base_stock
    
    # Calculate target inventory level
    target_level = adjusted_base_stock + safety_stock
    
    # Calculate raw order amount
    raw_order = max(0, target_level * demand_adj_factor - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply exponential smoothing to order amount for stability
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = smoothing_factor * raw_order + (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.prev_order
        compute_order_amount.prev_order = smoothed_order
        order_amount = smoothed_order
    else:
        compute_order_amount.prev_order = raw_order
        order_amount = raw_order
    
    # Ensure integer order amount
    return order_amount

    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Estimate demand using recent pipeline orders with more robust calculation
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        recent_demand_estimate = sum(pipeline_orders) / len(pipeline_orders)
        # More conservative demand ratio with tighter bounds
        demand_ratio = min(1.2, max(0.8, recent_demand_estimate / 80))
        adjusted_base_stock = base_stock * (0.6 + pipeline_weight * demand_ratio)
    else:
        adjusted_base_stock = base_stock
    
    # Calculate target inventory level
    target_level = adjusted_base_stock + safety_stock
    
    # Calculate raw order amount
    raw_order = max(0, target_level * demand_adj_factor - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply exponential smoothing to order amount for stability
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = smoothing_factor * raw_order + (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.prev_order
        compute_order_amount.prev_order = smoothed_order
        order_amount = smoothed_order
    else:
        compute_order_amount.prev_order = raw_order
        order_amount = raw_order
    
    # Ensure integer order amount
    return order_amount

Mechanism: function_attributes
Train Objective: 9122.8

Description: The key improvements include: 1) Increased base_stock and safety_stock to better handle the observed demand patterns which frequently exceed 90 units. 2) Adjusted demand_smoothing and pipeline_weight ...

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    base_stock = 98.0  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 98.0, 'min': 85, 'max': 115, 'type': 'float'}
    safety_stock = 28.0  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 28.0, 'min': 20, 'max': 40, 'type': 'float'}
    demand_smoothing = 0.7  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.7, 'min': 0.4, 'max': 0.95, 'type': 'float'}
    pipeline_weight = 0.35  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.35, 'min': 0.1, 'max': 0.7, 'type': 'float'}
    smoothing_factor = 0.8  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.8, 'min': 0.5, 'max': 1.0, 'type': 'float'}
    
    # Calculate inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Calculate demand estimate using weighted average of recent pipeline orders
    demand_estimate = 0
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        # Use weighted average of recent orders for better demand estimation
        recent_orders = pipeline_orders[-min(4, len(pipeline_orders)):]
        weights = [demand_smoothing ** i for i in range(len(recent_orders), 0, -1)]
        weighted_avg = sum(w * o for w, o in zip(weights, recent_orders)) / sum(weights)
        demand_estimate = weighted_avg * pipeline_weight
    
    # Dynamic target level that adapts to demand patterns
    target_level = base_stock + safety_stock + demand_estimate
    
    # Order up to target level with smoothing to avoid over-reaction
    order_gap = target_level - inventory_position
    order_amount = max(0, order_gap)
    
    # Apply smoothing to reduce order volatility
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = smoothing_factor * order_amount + (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.prev_order
        order_amount = smoothed_order
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = order_amount
    
    # Apply integer rounding for discrete demands
    order_amount = int(round(order_amount))
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    base_stock = 105.0  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 105.0, 'min': 90, 'max': 120, 'type': 'float'}
    safety_stock = 32.0  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 32.0, 'min': 25, 'max': 45, 'type': 'float'}
    demand_forecast_weight = 0.85  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.85, 'min': 0.6, 'max': 1.0, 'type': 'float'}
    pipeline_adjustment = 0.25  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.25, 'min': 0.1, 'max': 0.5, 'type': 'float'}
    smoothing_intensity = 0.9  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.9, 'min': 0.7, 'max': 1.0, 'type': 'float'}
    
    # Calculate total pipeline inventory
    total_pipeline = sum(pipeline_orders)
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + total_pipeline
    
    # Calculate demand forecast using recent pipeline orders
    demand_forecast = 0
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        # Use more recent orders with higher weights for better responsiveness
        recent_count = min(3, len(pipeline_orders))
        recent_orders = pipeline_orders[-recent_count:]
        weights = [demand_forecast_weight ** (recent_count - i - 1) for i in range(recent_count)]
        weighted_avg = sum(w * o for w, o in zip(weights, recent_orders)) / sum(weights)
        demand_forecast = weighted_avg * pipeline_adjustment
    
    # Dynamic target level that responds to demand patterns
    target_level = base_stock + safety_stock + demand_forecast
    
    # Calculate order gap to target
    order_gap = target_level - inventory_position
    order_amount = max(0, order_gap)
    
    # Apply smoothing to maintain stability
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = smoothing_intensity * order_amount + (1 - smoothing_intensity) * compute_order_amount.prev_order
        order_amount = smoothed_order
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = order_amount
    
    # Round to nearest integer for discrete ordering
    order_amount = int(round(order_amount))

        smoothed_order = smoothing_intensity * order_amount + (1 - smoothing_intensity) * compute_order_amount.prev_order
        order_amount = smoothed_order

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    base_stock = 118.0  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 118.0, 'min': 95, 'max': 125, 'type': 'float'}
    safety_stock = 32.0  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 32.0, 'min': 20, 'max': 40, 'type': 'float'}
    demand_forecast_weight = 0.68  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.68, 'min': 0.5, 'max': 0.9, 'type': 'float'}
    pipeline_adjustment = 0.12  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.12, 'min': 0.05, 'max': 0.3, 'type': 'float'}
    smoothing_intensity = 0.75  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.75, 'min': 0.6, 'max': 0.95, 'type': 'float'}
    demand_variance_factor = 0.42  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.42, 'min': 0.1, 'max': 0.6, 'type': 'float'}
    
    # Calculate total pipeline inventory
    total_pipeline = sum(pipeline_orders)
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + total_pipeline
    
    # Calculate demand forecast using recent pipeline orders with exponential smoothing
    demand_forecast = 0
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        # Use more recent orders with higher weights for better responsiveness
        recent_count = min(4, len(pipeline_orders))
        recent_orders = pipeline_orders[-recent_count:]
        weights = [demand_forecast_weight ** (recent_count - i - 1) for i in range(recent_count)]
        weighted_avg = sum(w * o for w, o in zip(weights, recent_orders)) / sum(weights)
        demand_forecast = weighted_avg * pipeline_adjustment
    
    # Calculate demand variability adjustment based on standard deviation
    if len(pipeline_orders) >= 3:
        recent_demands = pipeline_orders[-3:]
        mean_demand = sum(recent_demands) / len(recent_demands)
        variance = sum((d - mean_demand) ** 2 for d in recent_demands) / len(recent_demands)
        std_dev = variance ** 0.5
        variability_adjustment = std_dev * demand_variance_factor
    else:
        variability_adjustment = 0
    
    # Dynamic target level that responds to demand patterns and variability
    target_level = base_stock + safety_stock + demand_forecast + variability_adjustment
    
    # Calculate order gap to target
    order_gap = target_level - inventory_position
    order_amount = max(0, order_gap)
    
    # Apply smoothing to maintain stability while being responsive
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = smoothing_intensity * order_amount + (1 - smoothing_intensity) * compute_order_amount.prev_order
        order_amount = smoothed_order
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = order_amount
    
    # Round to nearest integer for discrete ordering
    order_amount = int(round(order_amount))
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    base_stock = 120.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 120.0, "min": 80.0, "max": 120.0, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock = 40.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 40.0, "min": 15.0, "max": 40.0, "type": "float"}
    demand_forecast = 82.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 82.0, "min": 60.0, "max": 100.0, "type": "float"}
    adjustment_factor = 0.5  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.5, "min": 0.3, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    min_order = 5.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 5.0, "min": 0.0, "max": 30.0, "type": "float"}
    max_order = 85.63469192582185  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 85.63469192582185, "min": 50.0, "max": 150.0, "type": "float"}
    
    smoothing_factor = 0.5  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.5, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.5, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Calculate target level with dynamic adjustment based on pipeline coverage
    target_level = base_stock + safety_stock
    
    # More responsive pipeline coverage calculation
    pipeline_coverage = sum(pipeline_orders) / demand_forecast if demand_forecast > 0 else 0
    
    # Progressive adjustment based on pipeline coverage
    if pipeline_coverage > 1.2:
        adjustment = 1.0 - (pipeline_coverage - 1.2) * adjustment_factor
        target_level *= max(0.6, adjustment)
    
    # Calculate order amount with smoothing
    raw_order = max(0, target_level - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply exponential smoothing to reduce order volatility
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = (smoothing_factor * raw_order + 
                        (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.prev_order)
    else:
        smoothed_order = raw_order
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = smoothed_order
    
    # Apply order constraints
    if smoothed_order > 0:
        order_amount = max(min_order, min(max_order, smoothed_order))
    else:
        order_amount = 0
    
    # Round to nearest integer
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    base_stock = 120.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 120.0, "min": 80.0, "max": 120.0, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock = 40.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 40.0, "min": 15.0, "max": 40.0, "type": "float"}
    demand_forecast = 82.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 82.0, "min": 60.0, "max": 100.0, "type": "float"}
    adjustment_factor = 0.5  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.5, "min": 0.3, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    min_order = 5.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 5.0, "min": 0.0, "max": 30.0, "type": "float"}
    max_order = 85.63469192582185  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 85.63469192582185, "min": 50.0, "max": 150.0, "type": "float"}
    
    smoothing_factor = 0.5  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.5, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.5, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Calculate target level with dynamic adjustment based on pipeline coverage
    target_level = base_stock + safety_stock
    
    # More responsive pipeline coverage calculation
    pipeline_coverage = sum(pipeline_orders) / demand_forecast if demand_forecast > 0 else 0
    
    # Progressive adjustment based on pipeline coverage
    if pipeline_coverage > 1.2:
        adjustment = 1.0 - (pipeline_coverage - 1.2) * adjustment_factor
        target_level *= max(0.6, adjustment)
    
    # Calculate order amount with smoothing
    raw_order = max(0, target_level - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply exponential smoothing to reduce order volatility
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = (smoothing_factor * raw_order + 
                        (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.prev_order)
    else:
        smoothed_order = raw_order
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = smoothed_order
    
    # Apply order constraints
    if smoothed_order > 0:
        order_amount = max(min_order, min(max_order, smoothed_order))
    else:
        order_amount = 0
    
    # Round to nearest integer
    return order_amount

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    base_stock = 88.28166104501817  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 88.28166104501817, "min": 80.0, "max": 120.0, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock = 20.52132900996712  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 20.52132900996712, "min": 15.0, "max": 40.0, "type": "float"}
    demand_forecast = 72.15045285997493  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 72.15045285997493, "min": 60.0, "max": 100.0, "type": "float"}
    adjustment_factor = 0.4  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.4, "min": 0.3, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    min_order = 5.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 5.0, "min": 0.0, "max": 30.0, "type": "float"}
    max_order = 110.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 110.0, "min": 50.0, "max": 150.0, "type": "float"}
    smoothing_factor = 0.49014387690109024  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.49014387690109024, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.5, "type": "float"}
    
    lead_time_coverage = 0.8455728933636992  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.8455728933636992, "min": 0.8, "max": 1.5, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Calculate target level with lead time consideration
    target_level = base_stock + safety_stock + (demand_forecast * lead_time_coverage)
    
    # Pipeline coverage adjustment - more conservative
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        pipeline_coverage = sum(pipeline_orders) / (demand_forecast * (len(pipeline_orders) + 1))
        if pipeline_coverage > 0.8:
            adjustment = max(0.6, 1.0 - (pipeline_coverage - 0.8) * adjustment_factor)
            target_level *= adjustment
    
    # Calculate raw order amount
    raw_order = max(0, target_level - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply exponential smoothing with reset mechanism
    if not hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        compute_order_amount.prev_order = raw_order
    
    smoothed_order = (smoothing_factor * raw_order + 
                    (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.prev_order)
    
    # Reset smoothing if there's a significant inventory gap
    if abs(raw_order - compute_order_amount.prev_order) > demand_forecast:
        smoothed_order = raw_order
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = smoothed_order
    
    # Apply order constraints with rounding to integer
    if smoothed_order > 0:
        order_amount = int(max(min_order, min(max_order, smoothed_order)))
    else:
        order_amount = 0
    
    return order_amount

Description: The original policy had a high holding cost relative to lost sales cost, indicating over-ordering. I reduced the base stock from 165 to 158 to better match the observed demand patterns. I increased th...

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    base_stock = 158  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 158, 'min': 140, 'max': 180, 'type': 'float'}
    safety_stock = 28  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 28, 'min': 20, 'max': 40, 'type': 'float'}
    demand_buffer = 15  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 15, 'min': 10, 'max': 25, 'type': 'float'}
    smoothing_factor = 0.7  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.7, 'min': 0.3, 'max': 0.9, 'type': 'float'}
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Calculate recent demand from pipeline orders (last 5 periods if available)
    recent_periods = min(5, len(pipeline_orders))
    if recent_periods > 0:
        recent_orders = pipeline_orders[:recent_periods]
        avg_recent_demand = sum(recent_orders) / len(recent_orders)
    else:
        avg_recent_demand = 80
    
    # Dynamic base stock adjustment with more granular demand response
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock + demand_buffer
    
    # More responsive demand-based adjustments
    if avg_recent_demand > 95:
        adjusted_base_stock += safety_stock * 1.5
    elif avg_recent_demand > 85:
        adjusted_base_stock += safety_stock * 1.0
    elif avg_recent_demand < 60:
        adjusted_base_stock -= safety_stock * 0.8
    elif avg_recent_demand < 70:
        adjusted_base_stock -= safety_stock * 0.4
    
    # Calculate target order with smoothing
    target_order = max(0, adjusted_base_stock - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply exponential smoothing to reduce order volatility
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'last_order'):
        smoothed_order = (smoothing_factor * target_order + 
                        (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.last_order)
    else:
        smoothed_order = target_order
    
    compute_order_amount.last_order = smoothed_order
    
    # More aggressive capping for large orders to reduce holding costs
    if smoothed_order > 90:
        order_amount = min(smoothed_order, 110)
    else:
        order_amount = smoothed_order
    
    return order_amount

Experiment: deepseek-chat_normal0_80_50_50_plain_processed_no_15_default_m3_r4

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    base_stock = 145  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 145, 'min': 130, 'max': 160, 'type': 'float'}
    safety_stock = 22  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 22, 'min': 15, 'max': 30, 'type': 'float'}
    smoothing_factor = 0.85  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.85, 'min': 0.6, 'max': 0.95, 'type': 'float'}
    demand_response_factor = 0.8  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.8, 'min': 0.5, 'max': 1.2, 'type': 'float'}
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Use recent pipeline orders as demand proxy (last 3 periods for more responsiveness)
    recent_periods = min(3, len(pipeline_orders))
    if recent_periods > 0:
        recent_orders = pipeline_orders[:recent_periods]
        avg_recent_demand = sum(recent_orders) / len(recent_orders)
    else:
        avg_recent_demand = 80
    
    # Dynamic base stock adjustment based on recent demand trends
    demand_deviation = avg_recent_demand - 80  # 80 is approximate mean demand
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock + (demand_deviation * demand_response_factor)
    
    # Add safety stock with more conservative approach
    adjusted_base_stock += safety_stock
    
    # Calculate target order
    target_order = max(0, adjusted_base_stock - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply exponential smoothing to reduce volatility
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'last_order'):
        smoothed_order = (smoothing_factor * target_order + 
                        (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.last_order)
    else:
        smoothed_order = target_order
    
    compute_order_amount.last_order = smoothed_order
    
    # Conservative order capping to prevent over-ordering
    if smoothed_order > 100:
        order_amount = min(smoothed_order, 95)
    else:
        order_amount = smoothed_order
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    base_stock = 145  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 145, 'min': 130, 'max': 160, 'type': 'float'}
    safety_stock = 22  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 22, 'min': 15, 'max': 30, 'type': 'float'}
    smoothing_factor = 0.85  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.85, 'min': 0.6, 'max': 0.95, 'type': 'float'}
    demand_response_factor = 0.8  # OPT_PARAM: {'initial': 0.8, 'min': 0.5, 'max': 1.2, 'type': 'float'}
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Use recent pipeline orders as demand proxy (last 3 periods for more responsiveness)
    recent_periods = min(3, len(pipeline_orders))
    if recent_periods > 0:
        recent_orders = pipeline_orders[:recent_periods]
        avg_recent_demand = sum(recent_orders) / len(recent_orders)
    else:
        avg_recent_demand = 80
    
    # Dynamic base stock adjustment based on recent demand trends
    demand_deviation = avg_recent_demand - 80  # 80 is approximate mean demand
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock + (demand_deviation * demand_response_factor)
    
    # Add safety stock with more conservative approach
    adjusted_base_stock += safety_stock
    
    # Calculate target order
    target_order = max(0, adjusted_base_stock - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply exponential smoothing to reduce volatility
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'last_order'):
        smoothed_order = (smoothing_factor * target_order + 
                        (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.last_order)
    else:
        smoothed_order = target_order
    
    compute_order_amount.last_order = smoothed_order
    
    # Conservative order capping to prevent over-ordering
    if smoothed_order > 100:
        order_amount = min(smoothed_order, 95)
    else:
        order_amount = smoothed_order
    
    return order_amount

POLICY #36
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Experiment: deepseek-chat_normal_std30_L2_c1_2_80_50_50_plain_no_scipy_15_default_m2_r1
Generation: 3
Mechanism: function_attributes
Train Objective: 2657.25007
Test Objective: 2553.17704

Description: The new policy introduces several improvements: 1) Dynamic base stock adjustment based on recent demand patterns using exponential smoothing, making it responsive to changing demand. 2) Pipeline-aware...

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Base stock level with safety stock adjustment
    base_stock = 170.9926818289656  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 170.9926818289656, "min": 100, "max": 500, "type": "float"}
    
    # Demand forecasting parameters
    forecast_horizon = 3  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 3, "min": 1, "max": 10, "type": "int"}
    smoothing_factor = 0.3  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.3, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.9, "type": "float"}
    
    # Pipeline adjustment factor
    pipeline_factor = 0.5  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.5, "min": 0.5, "max": 1.2, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    current_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Estimate future demand using exponential smoothing concept
    # For simplicity, we'll use a weighted average of recent pipeline arrivals
    recent_demand_estimate = 0
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        weights = [(1 - smoothing_factor) ** i for i in range(min(len(pipeline_orders), forecast_horizon))]
        weight_sum = sum(weights)
        recent_values = pipeline_orders[:forecast_horizon]
        recent_demand_estimate = sum(w * v for w, v in zip(weights, recent_values)) / weight_sum if weight_sum > 0 else 100
    
    # Adjust base stock based on demand pattern
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock * (1 + (recent_demand_estimate - 100) / 200)  # Normalize around typical demand
    
    # Calculate order amount with pipeline consideration
    pipeline_adjustment = sum(pipeline_orders) * pipeline_factor
    order_amount = max(0, adjusted_base_stock - current_position + pipeline_adjustment)
    
    # Apply smoothing to order quantities to avoid drastic changes
    order_smoothing = 1.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.0, "min": 0.3, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'previous_order'):
        smoothed_order = order_smoothing * compute_order_amount.previous_order + (1 - order_smoothing) * order_amount
        order_amount = max(0, smoothed_order)
    
    compute_order_amount.previous_order = order_amount
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Base stock level with safety stock adjustment
    base_stock = 170.9926818289656  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 170.9926818289656, "min": 100, "max": 500, "type": "float"}
    
    # Demand forecasting parameters
    forecast_horizon = 3  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 3, "min": 1, "max": 10, "type": "int"}
    smoothing_factor = 0.3  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.3, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.9, "type": "float"}
    
    # Pipeline adjustment factor
    pipeline_factor = 0.5  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.5, "min": 0.5, "max": 1.2, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    current_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Estimate future demand using exponential smoothing concept
    # For simplicity, we'll use a weighted average of recent pipeline arrivals
    recent_demand_estimate = 0
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        weights = [(1 - smoothing_factor) ** i for i in range(min(len(pipeline_orders), forecast_horizon))]
        weight_sum = sum(weights)
        recent_values = pipeline_orders[:forecast_horizon]
        recent_demand_estimate = sum(w * v for w, v in zip(weights, recent_values)) / weight_sum if weight_sum > 0 else 100
    
    # Adjust base stock based on demand pattern
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock * (1 + (recent_demand_estimate - 100) / 200)  # Normalize around typical demand
    
    # Calculate order amount with pipeline consideration
    pipeline_adjustment = sum(pipeline_orders) * pipeline_factor
    order_amount = max(0, adjusted_base_stock - current_position + pipeline_adjustment)
    
    # Apply smoothing to order quantities to avoid drastic changes
    order_smoothing = 1.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.0, "min": 0.3, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'previous_order'):
        smoothed_order = order_smoothing * compute_order_amount.previous_order + (1 - order_smoothing) * order_amount
        order_amount = max(0, smoothed_order)
    
    compute_order_amount.previous_order = order_amount
    
    return order_amount

Description: The new policy introduces several improvements: 1) Dynamic base stock adjustment based on recent demand patterns using exponential smoothing, making it responsive to changing demand. 2) Pipeline-aware...

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Base stock level with safety stock adjustment
    base_stock = 170.9926818289656  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 170.9926818289656, "min": 100, "max": 500, "type": "float"}
    
    # Demand forecasting parameters
    forecast_horizon = 3  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 3, "min": 1, "max": 10, "type": "int"}
    smoothing_factor = 0.3  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.3, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.9, "type": "float"}
    
    # Pipeline adjustment factor
    pipeline_factor = 0.5  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.5, "min": 0.5, "max": 1.2, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    current_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Estimate future demand using exponential smoothing concept
    # For simplicity, we'll use a weighted average of recent pipeline arrivals
    recent_demand_estimate = 0
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        weights = [(1 - smoothing_factor) ** i for i in range(min(len(pipeline_orders), forecast_horizon))]
        weight_sum = sum(weights)
        recent_values = pipeline_orders[:forecast_horizon]
        recent_demand_estimate = sum(w * v for w, v in zip(weights, recent_values)) / weight_sum if weight_sum > 0 else 100
    
    # Adjust base stock based on demand pattern
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock * (1 + (recent_demand_estimate - 100) / 200)  # Normalize around typical demand
    
    # Calculate order amount with pipeline consideration
    pipeline_adjustment = sum(pipeline_orders) * pipeline_factor
    order_amount = max(0, adjusted_base_stock - current_position + pipeline_adjustment)
    
    # Apply smoothing to order quantities to avoid drastic changes
    order_smoothing = 1.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.0, "min": 0.3, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'previous_order'):
        smoothed_order = order_smoothing * compute_order_amount.previous_order + (1 - order_smoothing) * order_amount
        order_amount = max(0, smoothed_order)
    
    compute_order_amount.previous_order = order_amount
    
    return order_amount

Test Objective: 2638.80511

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Multi-level base stock policy with demand forecasting
    base_stock_1 = 234.49725466334172  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 234.49725466334172, "min": 100, "max": 500, "type": "float"}
    base_stock_2 = 350.1  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 350.1, "min": 150, "max": 600, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock = 34.497254663342865  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 34.497254663342865, "min": 10, "max": 150, "type": "float"}
    smoothing_factor = 0.17080775902052564  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.17080775902052564, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.9, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Estimate demand based on pipeline orders (recent demand pattern)
    if len(pipeline_orders) >= 2:
        recent_demand_estimate = sum(pipeline_orders[-2:]) / 2
    else:
        recent_demand_estimate = 100.0  # default estimate
    
    # Adaptive base stock level based on demand pattern
    if recent_demand_estimate > 120:
        target_base_stock = base_stock_2
    else:
        target_base_stock = base_stock_1
    
    # Adjust for safety stock and recent demand
    adjusted_base_stock = target_base_stock + safety_stock
    
    # Smooth ordering to avoid large fluctuations
    order_amount = max(0, adjusted_base_stock - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply smoothing to prevent drastic order changes
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = (smoothing_factor * order_amount + 
                        (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.prev_order)
        order_amount = max(0, smoothed_order)
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = order_amount
    
    return order_amount

Generation: 9
Mechanism: function_attributes

Test Objective: 2585.58647

Description: The new policy introduces several structural improvements: 1) Three-tier base stock system that dynamically adapts to low/medium/high demand regimes with configurable thresholds, providing better dema...

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Base stock levels optimized for different demand regimes
    base_stock_low = 280.5  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 280.5, "min": 150, "max": 400, "type": "float"}
    base_stock_medium = 348.90752832757806  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 348.90752832757806, "min": 250, "max": 600, "type": "float"}
    base_stock_high = 550.7  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 550.7, "min": 400, "max": 800, "type": "float"}
    
    demand_threshold_low = 80.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 80.0, "min": 50, "max": 120, "type": "float"}
    demand_threshold_high = 130.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 130.0, "min": 100, "max": 180, "type": "float"}
    
    smoothing_factor = 0.5  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.5, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.5, "type": "float"}
    
    pipeline_weight = 0.9  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.9, "min": 0.3, "max": 0.9, "type": "float"}
    
    safety_stock_multiplier = 1.8  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.8, "min": 1.0, "max": 3.0, "type": "float"}
    
    recent_periods = 3  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 3, "min": 2, "max": 6, "type": "int"}
    
    volatility_buffer = 10.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 10.0, "min": 10, "max": 50, "type": "float"}
    
    max_order_change = 101.15026462983052  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 101.15026462983052, "min": 50, "max": 200, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate inventory position including pipeline
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Estimate recent demand from pipeline orders (more sophisticated approach)
    if len(pipeline_orders) >= recent_periods:
        recent_demands = pipeline_orders[-recent_periods:]
        avg_recent_demand = sum(recent_demands) / len(recent_demands)
        # Calculate demand volatility
        demand_volatility = max(recent_demands) - min(recent_demands) if len(recent_demands) > 1 else 0
    else:
        avg_recent_demand = 100.0
        demand_volatility = 0
    
    # Dynamic base stock selection based on demand level and volatility
    if avg_recent_demand < demand_threshold_low:
        base_stock = base_stock_low
    elif avg_recent_demand > demand_threshold_high:
        base_stock = base_stock_high
    else:
        base_stock = base_stock_medium
    
    # Adjust for demand volatility
    volatility_adjustment = demand_volatility * safety_stock_multiplier + volatility_buffer
    
    # Calculate target inventory position considering pipeline impact
    pipeline_impact = sum(pipeline_orders) * pipeline_weight
    target_inventory = base_stock + volatility_adjustment - pipeline_impact
    
    # Calculate raw order amount
    raw_order = max(0, target_inventory - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply smoothing with order change limits
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = (smoothing_factor * raw_order + 
                        (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.prev_order)
        # Limit maximum order change to prevent drastic fluctuations
        order_change = smoothed_order - compute_order_amount.prev_order
        if abs(order_change) > max_order_change:
            smoothed_order = compute_order_amount.prev_order + (max_order_change if order_change > 0 else -max_order_change)
        order_amount = max(0, smoothed_order)
    else:
        order_amount = raw_order
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = order_amount
    
    return order_amount

    # Multi-level base stock policy with demand forecasting
    base_stock_level = 256.43492083069174  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 256.43492083069174, "min": 100, "max": 500, "type": "float"}

Description: The improved policy introduces several key structural enhancements: 1) Dynamic demand sensing that adjusts base stock based on recent arrival quantities, 2) Emergency stock mechanism that activates vo...

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Multi-level demand-adaptive base stock policy with lead time compensation
    base_stock_level = 252.32053632401477  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 252.32053632401477, "min": 150, "max": 400, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock = 26.337085877305427  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 26.337085877305427, "min": 15, "max": 80, "type": "float"}
    demand_forecast_factor = 0.3817621878217704  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.3817621878217704, "min": 0.3, "max": 0.9, "type": "float"}
    pipeline_adjustment = 1.1271308341754966  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.1271308341754966, "min": 0.5, "max": 1.2, "type": "float"}
    volatility_buffer = 1.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.0, "min": 1.0, "max": 2.0, "type": "float"}
    
    order_smoothing = 0.35259699399398  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.35259699399398, "min": 0.2, "max": 0.8, "type": "float"}
    
    emergency_threshold = 45.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 45.0, "min": 20, "max": 100, "type": "float"}
    
    high_demand_multiplier = 1.1  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.1, "min": 1.1, "max": 1.8, "type": "float"}
    
    low_demand_multiplier = 0.6907581611406047  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.6907581611406047, "min": 0.5, "max": 0.95, "type": "float"}
    
    lead_time_compensation = 1.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.0, "min": 1.0, "max": 1.5, "type": "float"}
    
    recent_arrival_boost = 1.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.0, "min": 1.0, "max": 1.4, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate effective inventory position with lead time compensation
    weighted_pipeline = sum(pipeline_orders) * pipeline_adjustment
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + weighted_pipeline
    
    # Dynamic base stock adjustment based on recent arrivals and pipeline status
    base_adjustment = 1.0
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        recent_arrival = pipeline_orders[0]
        if recent_arrival > 120:  # High recent arrival indicates strong demand
            base_adjustment *= high_demand_multiplier
        elif recent_arrival < 60:  # Low recent arrival indicates weak demand
            base_adjustment *= low_demand_multiplier
            
        # Apply recent arrival boost for better responsiveness
        if recent_arrival > 0:
            base_adjustment *= recent_arrival_boost
    
    # Apply lead time compensation for better pipeline management
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock_level * base_adjustment * lead_time_compensation
    
    # Emergency stock adjustment for critically low inventory
    emergency_adjustment = 1.0
    if on_hand_inventory < emergency_threshold:
        emergency_adjustment = volatility_buffer
    
    # Calculate target inventory with all adjustments
    target_inventory = adjusted_base_stock * emergency_adjustment + safety_stock
    
    # Calculate raw order amount
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply demand-forecast-based smoothing
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        forecast_adjusted_order = (demand_forecast_factor * order_amount + 
                                 (1 - demand_forecast_factor) * compute_order_amount.prev_order)
        order_amount = max(0, forecast_adjusted_order)
    
    # Apply final smoothing to prevent drastic changes
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'smoothed_order'):
        final_order = (order_smoothing * order_amount + 
                     (1 - order_smoothing) * compute_order_amount.smoothed_order)
        order_amount = max(0, final_order)
        compute_order_amount.smoothed_order = order_amount
    else:
        compute_order_amount.smoothed_order = order_amount
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = order_amount
    return order_amount

Train Objective: 2666.52852
Test Objective: 2620.55013

Description: The improved policy introduces several key structural enhancements: 1) Dynamic demand sensing that adjusts base stock based on recent arrival quantities, 2) Emergency stock mechanism that activates vo...

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Multi-level demand-adaptive base stock policy with lead time compensation
    base_stock_level = 252.32053632401477  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 252.32053632401477, "min": 150, "max": 400, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock = 26.337085877305427  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 26.337085877305427, "min": 15, "max": 80, "type": "float"}
    demand_forecast_factor = 0.3817621878217704  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.3817621878217704, "min": 0.3, "max": 0.9, "type": "float"}
    pipeline_adjustment = 1.1271308341754966  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.1271308341754966, "min": 0.5, "max": 1.2, "type": "float"}
    volatility_buffer = 1.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.0, "min": 1.0, "max": 2.0, "type": "float"}
    
    order_smoothing = 0.35259699399398  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.35259699399398, "min": 0.2, "max": 0.8, "type": "float"}
    
    emergency_threshold = 45.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 45.0, "min": 20, "max": 100, "type": "float"}
    
    high_demand_multiplier = 1.1  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.1, "min": 1.1, "max": 1.8, "type": "float"}
    
    low_demand_multiplier = 0.6907581611406047  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.6907581611406047, "min": 0.5, "max": 0.95, "type": "float"}
    
    lead_time_compensation = 1.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.0, "min": 1.0, "max": 1.5, "type": "float"}
    
    recent_arrival_boost = 1.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.0, "min": 1.0, "max": 1.4, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate effective inventory position with lead time compensation
    weighted_pipeline = sum(pipeline_orders) * pipeline_adjustment
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + weighted_pipeline
    
    # Dynamic base stock adjustment based on recent arrivals and pipeline status
    base_adjustment = 1.0
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        recent_arrival = pipeline_orders[0]
        if recent_arrival > 120:  # High recent arrival indicates strong demand
            base_adjustment *= high_demand_multiplier
        elif recent_arrival < 60:  # Low recent arrival indicates weak demand
            base_adjustment *= low_demand_multiplier
            
        # Apply recent arrival boost for better responsiveness
        if recent_arrival > 0:
            base_adjustment *= recent_arrival_boost
    
    # Apply lead time compensation for better pipeline management
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock_level * base_adjustment * lead_time_compensation
    
    # Emergency stock adjustment for critically low inventory
    emergency_adjustment = 1.0
    if on_hand_inventory < emergency_threshold:
        emergency_adjustment = volatility_buffer
    
    # Calculate target inventory with all adjustments
    target_inventory = adjusted_base_stock * emergency_adjustment + safety_stock
    
    # Calculate raw order amount
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply demand-forecast-based smoothing
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        forecast_adjusted_order = (demand_forecast_factor * order_amount + 
                                 (1 - demand_forecast_factor) * compute_order_amount.prev_order)
        order_amount = max(0, forecast_adjusted_order)
    
    # Apply final smoothing to prevent drastic changes
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'smoothed_order'):
        final_order = (order_smoothing * order_amount + 
                     (1 - order_smoothing) * compute_order_amount.smoothed_order)
        order_amount = max(0, final_order)
        compute_order_amount.smoothed_order = order_amount
    else:
        compute_order_amount.smoothed_order = order_amount
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = order_amount
    return order_amount

Test Objective: 2614.29295

Description: I've redesigned the policy from a simple base-stock system to a multi-level inventory management approach with several key improvements: 1) Added safety stock to handle demand variability, 2) Introduc...

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Multi-level base stock policy with demand forecasting
    base_stock = 281.94312116985225  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 281.94312116985225, "min": 100, "max": 500, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock = 44.95632017308557  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 44.95632017308557, "min": 10, "max": 150, "type": "float"}
    demand_smoothing = 0.255085283900511  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.255085283900511, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.9, "type": "float"}
    pipeline_weight = 1.3753024999837313  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.3753024999837313, "min": 0.5, "max": 2.0, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate effective inventory position with weighted pipeline
    effective_pipeline = sum(pipeline_orders) * pipeline_weight
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + effective_pipeline
    
    # Dynamic base stock adjustment based on pipeline status
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0 and pipeline_orders[0] > 0:
        # Recent deliveries indicate higher demand volatility
        adjusted_base_stock = base_stock * 1.1
    else:
        adjusted_base_stock = base_stock
    
    # Calculate order amount with smoothing
    raw_order = max(0, adjusted_base_stock - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply smoothing to avoid drastic order changes
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = (demand_smoothing * raw_order + 
                        (1 - demand_smoothing) * compute_order_amount.prev_order)
    else:
        smoothed_order = raw_order
        compute_order_amount.prev_order = smoothed_order
    
    # Ensure minimum order quantity for efficiency
    if smoothed_order < safety_stock:
        order_amount = safety_stock
    else:
        order_amount = max(0, int(round(smoothed_order)))
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = order_amount
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Multi-level base stock policy with demand forecasting
    base_stock = 281.94312116985225  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 281.94312116985225, "min": 100, "max": 500, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock = 44.95632017308557  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 44.95632017308557, "min": 10, "max": 150, "type": "float"}
    demand_smoothing = 0.255085283900511  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.255085283900511, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.9, "type": "float"}
    pipeline_weight = 1.3753024999837313  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.3753024999837313, "min": 0.5, "max": 2.0, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate effective inventory position with weighted pipeline
    effective_pipeline = sum(pipeline_orders) * pipeline_weight
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + effective_pipeline
    
    # Dynamic base stock adjustment based on pipeline status
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0 and pipeline_orders[0] > 0:
        # Recent deliveries indicate higher demand volatility
        adjusted_base_stock = base_stock * 1.1
    else:
        adjusted_base_stock = base_stock
    
    # Calculate order amount with smoothing
    raw_order = max(0, adjusted_base_stock - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply smoothing to avoid drastic order changes
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = (demand_smoothing * raw_order + 
                        (1 - demand_smoothing) * compute_order_amount.prev_order)
    else:
        smoothed_order = raw_order
        compute_order_amount.prev_order = smoothed_order
    
    # Ensure minimum order quantity for efficiency
    if smoothed_order < safety_stock:
        order_amount = safety_stock
    else:
        order_amount = max(0, int(round(smoothed_order)))
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = order_amount
    
    return order_amount

import numpy as np

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Core policy parameters
    base_stock_level = 217.2619121985305  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 217.2619121985305, "min": 200, "max": 400, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock_multiplier = 0.8  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.8, "min": 0.8, "max": 2.0, "type": "float"}
    
    # Demand-responsive adjustments
    demand_volatility_factor = 0.1  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.1, "min": 0.1, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    pipeline_risk_weight = 0.1  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.1, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.8, "type": "float"}
    
    # Dynamic adjustment parameters
    inventory_coverage_days = 1.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.0, "min": 1.0, "max": 3.0, "type": "float"}
    lost_sales_penalty_factor = 0.3  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.3, "min": 0.3, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate inventory position and pipeline risk
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    pipeline_risk = sum(pipeline_orders[i] * (1 - i/len(pipeline_orders)) for i in range(len(pipeline_orders)))
    
    # Dynamic safety stock based on demand volatility and pipeline risk
    safety_stock = base_stock_level * safety_stock_multiplier * demand_volatility_factor
    safety_stock += pipeline_risk * pipeline_risk_weight
    
    # Target inventory level with lost-sales penalty consideration
    target_inventory = base_stock_level + safety_stock
    target_inventory *= (1 + lost_sales_penalty_factor * (34.67 / (19.93 + 34.67)))  # Weight by actual cost ratio
    
    # Calculate order amount with coverage consideration
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply inventory coverage constraint
    expected_demand_cover = inventory_coverage_days * 100  # Rough estimate from demand patterns
    order_amount = min(order_amount, expected_demand_cover)
    
    # Smooth ordering to avoid extreme fluctuations
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'previous_order'):
        smoothing_factor = 0.8  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.8, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.8, "type": "float"}
        order_amount = smoothing_factor * order_amount + (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.previous_order
    
    compute_order_amount.previous_order = order_amount
    
    return order_amount

    # Multi-level base stock policy with demand forecasting
    base_stock_level = 257.1958051755677  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 257.1958051755677, "min": 100, "max": 500, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock = 17.45101401774381  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 17.45101401774381, "min": 10, "max": 150, "type": "float"}
    demand_smoothing_factor = 0.16676890613268042  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.16676890613268042, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.9, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Estimate expected demand based on recent pipeline orders (proxy for recent demand)
    if len(pipeline_orders) >= 2:
        recent_demand_estimate = sum(pipeline_orders[:2]) / 2.0
    else:
        recent_demand_estimate = 100.0  # Default estimate
    
    # Dynamic base stock adjustment based on demand pattern
    dynamic_base_stock = base_stock_level + safety_stock
    
    # If recent demand is high, increase base stock temporarily
    if recent_demand_estimate > 120:
        dynamic_base_stock += 20.0
    
    # Calculate order amount with smoothing to avoid extreme fluctuations
    raw_order = max(0, dynamic_base_stock - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply smoothing to order quantity
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = (demand_smoothing_factor * raw_order + 
                        (1 - demand_smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.prev_order)
    else:
        smoothed_order = raw_order
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = smoothed_order
    
    # Ensure order is integer and non-negative
    order_amount = max(0, int(round(smoothed_order)))
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Multi-level demand-adaptive policy with lead time compensation
    base_stock_level = 247.404139623713  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 247.404139623713, "min": 200, "max": 400, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock_multiplier = 0.6034774461282374  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.6034774461282374, "min": 0.5, "max": 3.0, "type": "float"}
    demand_forecast_horizon = 3  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 3, "min": 1, "max": 10, "type": "int"}
    lead_time_compensation = 0.14235731651416153  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.14235731651416153, "min": 0.1, "max": 2.0, "type": "float"}
    smoothing_factor = 0.15751623806622925  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.15751623806622925, "min": 0.05, "max": 0.5, "type": "float"}
    
    emergency_threshold = 45.7  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 45.7, "min": 20, "max": 100, "type": "float"}
    emergency_multiplier = 1.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.0, "min": 1.0, "max": 3.0, "type": "float"}
    
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Calculate demand forecast using pipeline orders as proxy for recent demand
    if len(pipeline_orders) >= demand_forecast_horizon:
        recent_demands = pipeline_orders[:demand_forecast_horizon]
        demand_forecast = sum(recent_demands) / len(recent_demands)
    else:
        demand_forecast = 110.0  # Default based on historical average
    
    # Dynamic safety stock based on demand variability and lead time
    demand_variability = max(20.0, demand_forecast * 0.25)  # Conservative variability estimate
    safety_stock = safety_stock_multiplier * demand_variability * (lead_time_compensation + 1)
    
    # Emergency ordering logic for low inventory situations
    if inventory_position < emergency_threshold:
        target_inventory = base_stock_level + emergency_multiplier * safety_stock
    else:
        target_inventory = base_stock_level + safety_stock
    
    # Calculate raw order amount
    raw_order = max(0, target_inventory - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply exponential smoothing to order quantities
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = (smoothing_factor * raw_order + 
                        (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.prev_order)
    else:
        smoothed_order = raw_order
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = smoothed_order
    
    # Ensure order is integer and non-negative
    order_amount = max(0, int(round(smoothed_order)))
    
    return order_amount

Generation: 8
Mechanism: function_attributes

Test Objective: 2633.31

Description: The original policy had imbalanced holding and lost-sales costs (20:35 ratio), indicating insufficient inventory coverage. Key improvements: 1) Increased base stock level to better match demand patter...

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Multi-level inventory policy with demand forecasting and lead time compensation
    base_stock_level = 251.0605660875973  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 251.0605660875973, "min": 200, "max": 400, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock_factor = 0.5580139790986705  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.5580139790986705, "min": 0.5, "max": 3.0, "type": "float"}
    demand_forecast_window = 5  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 5, "min": 3, "max": 10, "type": "int"}
    lead_time_compensation = 1.053217427310646  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.053217427310646, "min": 1.0, "max": 3.0, "type": "float"}
    smoothing_alpha = 0.1602026404599061  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.1602026404599061, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.5, "type": "float"}
    
    # Emergency ordering parameters
    emergency_threshold = 80.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 80.0, "min": 50, "max": 150, "type": "float"}
    emergency_multiplier = 1.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.0, "min": 1.0, "max": 2.5, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate inventory position (current + pipeline)
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Use pipeline orders as proxy for recent demand history
    if len(pipeline_orders) >= demand_forecast_window:
        recent_demands = pipeline_orders[:demand_forecast_window]
        avg_demand = sum(recent_demands) / len(recent_demands)
        # Estimate demand variability using standard deviation
        demand_variance = sum((d - avg_demand) ** 2 for d in recent_demands) / len(recent_demands)
        demand_std = max(15.0, demand_variance ** 0.5)
    else:
        avg_demand = 110.0  # Historical average fallback
        demand_std = 30.0   # Conservative variability estimate
    
    # Calculate safety stock considering lead time and demand variability
    safety_stock = safety_stock_factor * demand_std * (lead_time_compensation ** 0.5)
    
    # Emergency ordering for critical inventory situations
    if inventory_position < emergency_threshold:
        target_level = base_stock_level + emergency_multiplier * safety_stock
    else:
        target_level = base_stock_level + safety_stock
    
    # Calculate raw order quantity
    raw_order = max(0, target_level - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply exponential smoothing to reduce order volatility
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'previous_order'):
        smoothed_order = smoothing_alpha * raw_order + (1 - smoothing_alpha) * compute_order_amount.previous_order
    else:
        smoothed_order = raw_order
    
    compute_order_amount.previous_order = smoothed_order
    
    # Round to nearest integer and ensure non-negative
    order_amount = max(0, int(round(smoothed_order)))
    
    return order_amount

    # Adaptive demand forecasting using pipeline orders as demand proxy
    if len(pipeline_orders) >= demand_forecast_window:
        recent_demands = pipeline_orders[:demand_forecast_window]
        avg_demand = sum(recent_demands) / len(recent_demands)
        # Calculate demand variability with penalty for high volatility
        demand_variance = sum((d - avg_demand) ** 2 for d in recent_demands) / len(recent_demands)
        demand_std = max(20.0, demand_variance ** 0.5)
        cv = demand_std / avg_demand if avg_demand > 0 else 1.0
    else:
        avg_demand = 110.0
        demand_std = 35.0
        cv = 0.32
    
    # Risk-adjusted safety stock considering cost ratio (p/h = 2)
    # Higher penalty for lost sales suggests more aggressive safety stock
    risk_factor = cost_ratio_adjustment * (2.0 / 1.0)  # p/h ratio
    safety_stock = safety_stock_multiplier * demand_std * (lead_time_compensation ** 0.5) * risk_factor
    
    # Adjust base stock for demand variability
    variability_adjustment = 1.0 + variability_penalty * cv
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock_level * variability_adjustment
    
    # Emergency ordering with boost for critical situations
    if inventory_position < emergency_threshold:
        target_level = adjusted_base_stock + emergency_boost * safety_stock
    else:
        target_level = adjusted_base_stock + safety_stock
    
    # Calculate order quantity
    raw_order = max(0, target_level - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply smoothing to reduce order volatility
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'previous_order'):
        smoothed_order = smoothing_factor * raw_order + (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.previous_order
    else:
        smoothed_order = raw_order
    
    compute_order_amount.previous_order = smoothed_order
    
    # Round to nearest integer
    order_amount = max(0, int(round(smoothed_order)))
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Demand forecasting with exponential smoothing
    global demand_forecast
    if 'demand_forecast' not in globals():
        demand_forecast = 100.0  # Initial forecast
    alpha = 0.2  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.2, "min": 0.05, "max": 0.5, "type": "float"}
    
    # Base stock policy parameters
    base_stock_level = 450.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 450.0, "min": 300, "max": 800, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock = 81.99949944615614  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 81.99949944615614, "min": 50, "max": 150, "type": "float"}
    
    # Pipeline and inventory management
    pipeline_total = sum(pipeline_orders)
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + pipeline_total
    
    # Calculate target inventory level based on demand forecast and lead time
    lead_time_demand = demand_forecast * 2.5  # L=2 plus buffer
    target_inventory = lead_time_demand + safety_stock
    
    # Order quantity calculation with smoothing
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply order smoothing and limits
    smoothing_factor = 0.6  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.6, "min": 0.3, "max": 0.9, "type": "float"}
    max_order_quantity = 150.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 150.0, "min": 150, "max": 400, "type": "float"}
    
    order_amount = min(order_amount * smoothing_factor + 
                     (1 - smoothing_factor) * min(order_amount, max_order_quantity),
                     max_order_quantity)
    
    # Update demand forecast for next period (simplified - would need actual demand)
    # This is a placeholder - in practice we'd need the current period's demand
    demand_forecast = demand_forecast  # Will be updated externally
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    base_stock = 523.7257054239889  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 523.7257054239889, "min": 400, "max": 800, "type": "float"}
    demand_smoothing = 0.8  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.8, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.8, "type": "float"}
    safety_factor = 1.5  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.5, "min": 1.0, "max": 3.0, "type": "float"}
    order_smoothing = 0.30000000000000004  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.30000000000000004, "min": 0.2, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    pipeline_adjustment = 0.5  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.5, "min": 0.5, "max": 1.2, "type": "float"}
    
    lead_time = 2
    
    # Calculate inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Estimate demand using exponential smoothing of pipeline orders
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        # Use weighted average of recent orders as demand estimate
        weights = [0.6, 0.4] if len(pipeline_orders) >= 2 else [1.0]
        recent_orders = pipeline_orders[-len(weights):]
        demand_estimate = sum(w * o for w, o in zip(weights, recent_orders))
        
        # Smooth the demand estimate
        if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_demand_estimate'):
            demand_estimate = (demand_smoothing * demand_estimate + 
                            (1 - demand_smoothing) * compute_order_amount.prev_demand_estimate)
        compute_order_amount.prev_demand_estimate = demand_estimate
    else:
        demand_estimate = 100.0
    
    # Calculate lead time demand with safety buffer
    lead_time_demand = demand_estimate * lead_time
    safety_stock = safety_factor * (demand_estimate ** 0.7)  # Non-linear safety stock
    
    # Adjust base stock based on demand variability
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock * (0.9 + 0.1 * (demand_estimate / 120.0))
    
    # Calculate target inventory position
    target_inventory = max(adjusted_base_stock, lead_time_demand + safety_stock)
    
    # Adjust for pipeline orders more aggressively
    pipeline_effect = pipeline_adjustment * sum(pipeline_orders)
    net_target = target_inventory - pipeline_effect
    
    # Calculate order quantity with smoothing
    raw_order = net_target - on_hand_inventory
    
    if raw_order > 0:
        order_amount = order_smoothing * raw_order
    else:
        order_amount = 0
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    base_stock = 544.8127746156831  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 544.8127746156831, "min": 400, "max": 900, "type": "float"}
    demand_smoothing = 0.1517635754542214  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.1517635754542214, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.8, "type": "float"}
    safety_factor = 2.2  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 2.2, "min": 1.5, "max": 4.0, "type": "float"}
    order_smoothing = 0.3  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.3, "min": 0.3, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    pipeline_weight = 0.8  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.8, "min": 0.5, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    variability_factor = 1.8  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.8, "min": 1.0, "max": 3.0, "type": "float"}
    
    lead_time = 2
    
    # Calculate inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Enhanced demand estimation using recent pipeline orders and exponential smoothing
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        # Use more sophisticated weighting for demand estimation
        if len(pipeline_orders) >= 2:
            weights = [0.7, 0.3]  # More weight on most recent order
            recent_orders = pipeline_orders[-2:]
            demand_estimate = sum(w * o for w, o in zip(weights, recent_orders))
        else:
            demand_estimate = pipeline_orders[-1]
        
        # Apply exponential smoothing with memory
        if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_demand_estimate'):
            smoothed_demand = (demand_smoothing * demand_estimate + 
                            (1 - demand_smoothing) * compute_order_amount.prev_demand_estimate)
            compute_order_amount.prev_demand_estimate = smoothed_demand
            demand_estimate = smoothed_demand
        else:
            compute_order_amount.prev_demand_estimate = demand_estimate
    else:
        demand_estimate = 110.0  # Based on historical average
    
    # Calculate safety stock considering demand variability
    safety_stock = safety_factor * (demand_estimate ** 0.5) * variability_factor
    
    # Dynamic base stock adjustment based on demand patterns
    if demand_estimate > 130:  # High demand period
        adjusted_base_stock = base_stock * 1.15
    elif demand_estimate < 80:  # Low demand period  
        adjusted_base_stock = base_stock * 0.9
    else:
        adjusted_base_stock = base_stock
    
    # Calculate target inventory position with lead time demand
    lead_time_demand = demand_estimate * lead_time
    target_inventory = lead_time_demand + safety_stock
    
    # Ensure minimum base stock level
    target_inventory = max(target_inventory, adjusted_base_stock)
    
    # Account for pipeline orders with weighted adjustment
    pipeline_adjustment = pipeline_weight * sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Calculate required order
    required_order = target_inventory - inventory_position
    
    # Apply order smoothing and ensure non-negative
    if required_order > 0:
        order_amount = order_smoothing * required_order
    else:
        order_amount = 0
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Multi-level base stock policy with demand forecasting
    base_stock_level = 317.7476468960112  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 317.7476468960112, "min": 100, "max": 600, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock = 47.74764689600181  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 47.74764689600181, "min": 20, "max": 200, "type": "float"}
    demand_smoothing_factor = 0.8  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.8, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.8, "type": "float"}
    pipeline_coverage_factor = 0.8  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.8, "min": 0.8, "max": 2.0, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Estimate expected demand based on recent pipeline arrivals
    recent_demand_estimate = sum(pipeline_orders[:2]) / 2 if len(pipeline_orders) >= 2 else pipeline_orders[0] if pipeline_orders else 100
    
    # Dynamic base stock adjustment based on demand pattern
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock_level + safety_stock
    
    # Calculate order amount with smoothing and pipeline consideration
    target_inventory = adjusted_base_stock + pipeline_coverage_factor * recent_demand_estimate
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply smoothing to avoid drastic order changes
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = demand_smoothing_factor * order_amount + (1 - demand_smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.prev_order
        order_amount = max(0, smoothed_order)
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = order_amount
    
    return order_amount

1. **Dynamic Safety Stock**: Instead of a fixed safety stock, it now calculates safety stock based on demand variability observed in pipeline orders, making ...

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Multi-level base stock policy with demand forecasting and variance adjustment
    base_stock_level = 310.134656543853  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 310.134656543853, "min": 200, "max": 600, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock_multiplier = 0.5  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.5, "min": 0.5, "max": 3.0, "type": "float"}
    demand_forecast_horizon = 3  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 3, "min": 1, "max": 10, "type": "int"}
    pipeline_weight = 0.27439347691898913  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.27439347691898913, "min": 0.1, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    smoothing_factor = 0.11144715467754478  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.11144715467754478, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.9, "type": "float"}
    variance_factor = 0.1  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.1, "min": 0.1, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    
    lead_time = 4
    
    # Calculate inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Estimate demand using exponential smoothing of pipeline arrivals
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        weights = [0.5 ** (i + 1) for i in range(min(demand_forecast_horizon, len(pipeline_orders)))]
        total_weight = sum(weights)
        weighted_demand = sum(w * pipeline_orders[i] for i, w in enumerate(weights))
        demand_forecast = weighted_demand / total_weight
    else:
        demand_forecast = 100.0  # Default estimate
    
    # Calculate demand variance from pipeline for safety stock
    if len(pipeline_orders) >= 2:
        recent_demands = pipeline_orders[:min(5, len(pipeline_orders))]
        demand_variance = max(0, (max(recent_demands) - min(recent_demands)) / 2)
    else:
        demand_variance = 20.0  # Default variance
    
    # Dynamic safety stock based on demand variability
    safety_stock = safety_stock_multiplier * demand_variance * variance_factor
    
    # Adjust base stock for lead time demand
    lead_time_demand = demand_forecast * lead_time
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock_level + lead_time_demand * pipeline_weight + safety_stock
    
    # Calculate order amount
    target_inventory = adjusted_base_stock
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply smoothing to maintain order stability
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = smoothing_factor * order_amount + (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.prev_order
        order_amount = max(0, smoothed_order)
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = order_amount
    
    # Round to nearest integer for practical ordering
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Multi-level base stock policy with demand forecasting and variance adjustment
    base_stock_level = 310.134656543853  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 310.134656543853, "min": 200, "max": 600, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock_multiplier = 0.5  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.5, "min": 0.5, "max": 3.0, "type": "float"}
    demand_forecast_horizon = 3  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 3, "min": 1, "max": 10, "type": "int"}
    pipeline_weight = 0.27439347691898913  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.27439347691898913, "min": 0.1, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    smoothing_factor = 0.11144715467754478  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.11144715467754478, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.9, "type": "float"}
    variance_factor = 0.1  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.1, "min": 0.1, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    
    lead_time = 4
    
    # Calculate inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Estimate demand using exponential smoothing of pipeline arrivals
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        weights = [0.5 ** (i + 1) for i in range(min(demand_forecast_horizon, len(pipeline_orders)))]
        total_weight = sum(weights)
        weighted_demand = sum(w * pipeline_orders[i] for i, w in enumerate(weights))
        demand_forecast = weighted_demand / total_weight
    else:
        demand_forecast = 100.0  # Default estimate
    
    # Calculate demand variance from pipeline for safety stock
    if len(pipeline_orders) >= 2:
        recent_demands = pipeline_orders[:min(5, len(pipeline_orders))]
        demand_variance = max(0, (max(recent_demands) - min(recent_demands)) / 2)
    else:
        demand_variance = 20.0  # Default variance
    
    # Dynamic safety stock based on demand variability
    safety_stock = safety_stock_multiplier * demand_variance * variance_factor
    
    # Adjust base stock for lead time demand
    lead_time_demand = demand_forecast * lead_time
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock_level + lead_time_demand * pipeline_weight + safety_stock
    
    # Calculate order amount
    target_inventory = adjusted_base_stock
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply smoothing to maintain order stability
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = smoothing_factor * order_amount + (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.prev_order
        order_amount = max(0, smoothed_order)
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = order_amount
    
    # Round to nearest integer for practical ordering
    return order_amount
