POLICY #97
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Experiment: deepseek-chat_normal_std30_L4_c1_2_80_50_50_plain_no_scipy_15_default_m2_r4
Generation: 6
Mechanism: function_attributes
Train Objective: 3500.49802
Test Objective: 3421.98073

Description: ```

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Multi-level base stock policy with demand forecasting and dynamic safety stock
    base_stock_level = 224.67595136227277  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 224.67595136227277, "min": 200, "max": 600, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock_multiplier = 0.575650576927524  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.575650576927524, "min": 0.5, "max": 3.0, "type": "float"}
    demand_forecast_horizon = 5  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 5, "min": 1, "max": 10, "type": "int"}
    lead_time_weight = 0.5864238915997532  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.5864238915997532, "min": 0.1, "max": 1.5, "type": "float"}
    smoothing_factor = 0.11853239795026956  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.11853239795026956, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.9, "type": "float"}
    variance_penalty = 0.05  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.05, "min": 0.05, "max": 0.5, "type": "float"}
    service_level_factor = 0.6437786017800383  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.6437786017800383, "min": 0.5, "max": 3.0, "type": "float"}
    
    lead_time = 4
    
    # Calculate inventory position including pipeline
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Enhanced demand forecasting using exponential weighted moving average
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        recent_orders = pipeline_orders[:min(demand_forecast_horizon, len(pipeline_orders))]
        weights = [0.7 ** i for i in range(len(recent_orders))]  # More weight to recent orders
        total_weight = sum(weights)
        weighted_demand = sum(w * recent_orders[i] for i, w in enumerate(weights))
        demand_forecast = weighted_demand / total_weight
    else:
        demand_forecast = 100.0  # Default estimate
    
    # Calculate demand variability for safety stock
    if len(pipeline_orders) >= 3:
        recent_demands = pipeline_orders[:min(8, len(pipeline_orders))]
        mean_demand = sum(recent_demands) / len(recent_demands)
        squared_errors = [(d - mean_demand) ** 2 for d in recent_demands]
        demand_std = (sum(squared_errors) / len(squared_errors)) ** 0.5
    else:
        demand_std = 25.0  # Default standard deviation
    
    # Dynamic safety stock considering service level and demand variability
    safety_stock = safety_stock_multiplier * demand_std * service_level_factor
    
    # Lead time demand adjustment with variable weight
    lead_time_demand = demand_forecast * lead_time
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock_level + lead_time_demand * lead_time_weight + safety_stock
    
    # Calculate order amount with variance penalty for high variability periods
    target_inventory = adjusted_base_stock
    if demand_std > 40:  # High variability period
        target_inventory += variance_penalty * demand_std
    
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply smoothing with adaptive factor based on demand stability
    current_smoothing = smoothing_factor
    if demand_std > 50:  # Less smoothing during high variability
        current_smoothing = min(0.5, smoothing_factor * 1.5)
    
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = current_smoothing * order_amount + (1 - current_smoothing) * compute_order_amount.prev_order
        order_amount = max(0, smoothed_order)
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = order_amount
    
    # Round to nearest integer for practical ordering
    return order_amount

        order_amount = max(0, smoothed_order)
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = order_amount
    
    # Round to nearest integer for practical ordering
    return order_amount

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Multi-level base stock policy with demand forecasting and dynamic safety stock
    base_stock_level = 224.67595136227277  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 224.67595136227277, "min": 200, "max": 600, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock_multiplier = 0.575650576927524  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.575650576927524, "min": 0.5, "max": 3.0, "type": "float"}
    demand_forecast_horizon = 5  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 5, "min": 1, "max": 10, "type": "int"}
    lead_time_weight = 0.5864238915997532  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.5864238915997532, "min": 0.1, "max": 1.5, "type": "float"}
    smoothing_factor = 0.11853239795026956  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.11853239795026956, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.9, "type": "float"}
    variance_penalty = 0.05  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.05, "min": 0.05, "max": 0.5, "type": "float"}
    service_level_factor = 0.6437786017800383  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.6437786017800383, "min": 0.5, "max": 3.0, "type": "float"}
    
    lead_time = 4
    
    # Calculate inventory position including pipeline
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Enhanced demand forecasting using exponential weighted moving average
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        recent_orders = pipeline_orders[:min(demand_forecast_horizon, len(pipeline_orders))]
        weights = [0.7 ** i for i in range(len(recent_orders))]  # More weight to recent orders
        total_weight = sum(weights)
        weighted_demand = sum(w * recent_orders[i] for i, w in enumerate(weights))
        demand_forecast = weighted_demand / total_weight
    else:
        demand_forecast = 100.0  # Default estimate
    
    # Calculate demand variability for safety stock
    if len(pipeline_orders) >= 3:
        recent_demands = pipeline_orders[:min(8, len(pipeline_orders))]
        mean_demand = sum(recent_demands) / len(recent_demands)
        squared_errors = [(d - mean_demand) ** 2 for d in recent_demands]
        demand_std = (sum(squared_errors) / len(squared_errors)) ** 0.5
    else:
        demand_std = 25.0  # Default standard deviation
    
    # Dynamic safety stock considering service level and demand variability
    safety_stock = safety_stock_multiplier * demand_std * service_level_factor
    
    # Lead time demand adjustment with variable weight
    lead_time_demand = demand_forecast * lead_time
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock_level + lead_time_demand * lead_time_weight + safety_stock
    
    # Calculate order amount with variance penalty for high variability periods
    target_inventory = adjusted_base_stock
    if demand_std > 40:  # High variability period
        target_inventory += variance_penalty * demand_std
    
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply smoothing with adaptive factor based on demand stability
    current_smoothing = smoothing_factor
    if demand_std > 50:  # Less smoothing during high variability
        current_smoothing = min(0.5, smoothing_factor * 1.5)
    
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = current_smoothing * order_amount + (1 - current_smoothing) * compute_order_amount.prev_order
        order_amount = max(0, smoothed_order)
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = order_amount
    
    # Round to nearest integer for practical ordering
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Multi-level base stock policy with demand forecasting
    base_stock_level = 308.0504419895995  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 308.0504419895995, "min": 100, "max": 600, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock = 38.050441989587604  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 38.050441989587604, "min": 20, "max": 200, "type": "float"}
    demand_smoothing = 0.12558496738247107  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.12558496738247107, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.8, "type": "float"}
    
    pipeline_weight = 0.9560906196800674  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.9560906196800674, "min": 0.3, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    
    inventory_target = base_stock_level + safety_stock
    
    # Calculate effective pipeline (weighted by arrival time)
    weighted_pipeline = 0
    for i, order in enumerate(pipeline_orders):
        weight = pipeline_weight ** (i + 1)  # Higher weight for orders arriving sooner
        weighted_pipeline += order * weight
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    current_position = on_hand_inventory + weighted_pipeline
    
    # Order amount based on inventory position and target
    order_amount = max(0, inventory_target - current_position)
    
    # Apply smoothing to avoid large order fluctuations
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = (demand_smoothing * order_amount + 
                        (1 - demand_smoothing) * compute_order_amount.prev_order)
        order_amount = smoothed_order
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = order_amount
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Multi-level base stock policy with demand forecasting and adaptive safety stock
    base_stock_level = 283.42354076995707  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 283.42354076995707, "min": 150, "max": 400, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock_min = 10.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 10.0, "min": 10, "max": 80, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock_max = 120.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 120.0, "min": 30, "max": 120, "type": "float"}
    demand_alpha = 0.15  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.15, "min": 0.05, "max": 0.3, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate effective pipeline (unweighted sum of all pipeline orders)
    total_pipeline = sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    current_position = on_hand_inventory + total_pipeline
    
    # Adaptive safety stock based on pipeline variability
    pipeline_variability = max(1.0, sum(pipeline_orders) / len(pipeline_orders)) if pipeline_orders else 1.0
    adaptive_safety_stock = safety_stock_min + (safety_stock_max - safety_stock_min) * min(1.0, pipeline_variability / 50.0)
    
    # Calculate inventory target
    inventory_target = base_stock_level + adaptive_safety_stock
    
    # Order amount based on inventory position and target
    order_amount = max(0, inventory_target - current_position)
    
    # Maintain demand tracking for smoothing
    if not hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'demand_estimate'):
        compute_order_amount.demand_estimate = 100.0  # Initial estimate
    
    # Update demand estimate using exponential smoothing
    recent_demand = compute_order_amount.demand_estimate
    compute_order_amount.demand_estimate = (demand_alpha * recent_demand + 
                                         (1 - demand_alpha) * compute_order_amount.demand_estimate)
    
    # Apply order smoothing based on demand variability
    max_order_variation = compute_order_amount.demand_estimate * 0.5
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = compute_order_amount.prev_order * 0.7 + order_amount * 0.3
        # Limit order variation
        if abs(smoothed_order - compute_order_amount.prev_order) > max_order_variation:
            if smoothed_order > compute_order_amount.prev_order:
                smoothed_order = compute_order_amount.prev_order + max_order_variation
            else:
                smoothed_order = max(0, compute_order_amount.prev_order - max_order_variation)
        order_amount = smoothed_order
    
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = order_amount
    
    return order_amount

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Base stock level with lead time compensation
    base_stock_level = 320.546822199867  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 320.546822199867, "min": 250, "max": 400, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock = 45.546822199867115  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 45.546822199867115, "min": 20, "max": 80, "type": "float"}
    demand_smoothing_factor = 0.2  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.2, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.4, "type": "float"}
    pipeline_weight = 0.9  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.9, "min": 0.5, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    
    lost_sales_penalty_factor = 1.3  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.3, "min": 1.0, "max": 2.0, "type": "float"}
    
    order_smoothing = 0.9  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.9, "min": 0.3, "max": 0.9, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate effective pipeline (weighted by remaining lead time)
    weighted_pipeline = 0
    for i, order in enumerate(pipeline_orders):
        weight = pipeline_weight ** i  # Higher weight for orders arriving sooner
        weighted_pipeline += order * weight
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    current_position = on_hand_inventory + weighted_pipeline
    
    # Dynamic safety stock adjustment based on recent performance
    if not hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'recent_lost_sales'):
        compute_order_amount.recent_lost_sales = 0
        compute_order_amount.recent_periods = 0
    
    # Update lost sales tracking
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'last_on_hand') and compute_order_amount.last_on_hand is not None:
        if compute_order_amount.last_on_hand < 0:  # Had lost sales
            compute_order_amount.recent_lost_sales += 1
        compute_order_amount.recent_periods += 1
        
        # Reset tracking every 10 periods
        if compute_order_amount.recent_periods >= 10:
            lost_sales_ratio = compute_order_amount.recent_lost_sales / compute_order_amount.recent_periods
            dynamic_safety_adjustment = 1.0 + (lost_sales_ratio * lost_sales_penalty_factor)
            compute_order_amount.recent_lost_sales = 0
            compute_order_amount.recent_periods = 0
        else:
            dynamic_safety_adjustment = 1.0
    else:
        dynamic_safety_adjustment = 1.0
    
    # Calculate inventory target with dynamic safety stock
    inventory_target = base_stock_level + (safety_stock * dynamic_safety_adjustment)
    
    # Order amount based on inventory position and target
    raw_order_amount = max(0, inventory_target - current_position)
    
    # Maintain demand tracking for intelligent ordering
    if not hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'demand_estimate'):
        compute_order_amount.demand_estimate = 100.0
    
    # Update demand estimate using exponential smoothing
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'last_demand'):
        compute_order_amount.demand_estimate = (demand_smoothing_factor * compute_order_amount.last_demand + 
                                             (1 - demand_smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.demand_estimate)
    
    # Apply order smoothing to reduce volatility
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = (order_smoothing * compute_order_amount.prev_order + 
                        (1 - order_smoothing) * raw_order_amount)
        # Ensure order is reasonable relative to demand
        max_order = compute_order_amount.demand_estimate * 2.0
        smoothed_order = min(smoothed_order, max_order)
        order_amount = max(0, smoothed_order)
    else:
        order_amount = raw_order_amount
    
    # Store state for next period
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = order_amount
    compute_order_amount.last_on_hand = on_hand_inventory
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'last_demand'):
        compute_order_amount.last_demand = None  # Will be set externally
    
    return order_amount

POLICY #110
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Experiment: deepseek-chat_normal_std30_L4_c1_2_80_50_50_plain_processed_scipy_15_default_m2_r2
Generation: 9
Mechanism: function_attributes
Train Objective: 3696.50545
Test Objective: 3577.78107

Description: The original policy had high lost sales costs (53.71) compared to holding costs (24.23), indicating insufficient inventory. Key improvements: 1) Simplified base stock policy with weighted pipeline acc...

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Base stock level with lead time compensation
    base_stock_level = 320.546822199867  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 320.546822199867, "min": 250, "max": 400, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock = 45.546822199867115  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 45.546822199867115, "min": 20, "max": 80, "type": "float"}
    demand_smoothing_factor = 0.2  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.2, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.4, "type": "float"}
    pipeline_weight = 0.9  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.9, "min": 0.5, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    
    lost_sales_penalty_factor = 1.3  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.3, "min": 1.0, "max": 2.0, "type": "float"}
    
    order_smoothing = 0.9  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.9, "min": 0.3, "max": 0.9, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate effective pipeline (weighted by remaining lead time)
    weighted_pipeline = 0
    for i, order in enumerate(pipeline_orders):
        weight = pipeline_weight ** i  # Higher weight for orders arriving sooner
        weighted_pipeline += order * weight
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    current_position = on_hand_inventory + weighted_pipeline
    
    # Dynamic safety stock adjustment based on recent performance
    if not hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'recent_lost_sales'):
        compute_order_amount.recent_lost_sales = 0
        compute_order_amount.recent_periods = 0
    
    # Update lost sales tracking
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'last_on_hand') and compute_order_amount.last_on_hand is not None:
        if compute_order_amount.last_on_hand < 0:  # Had lost sales
            compute_order_amount.recent_lost_sales += 1
        compute_order_amount.recent_periods += 1
        
        # Reset tracking every 10 periods
        if compute_order_amount.recent_periods >= 10:
            lost_sales_ratio = compute_order_amount.recent_lost_sales / compute_order_amount.recent_periods
            dynamic_safety_adjustment = 1.0 + (lost_sales_ratio * lost_sales_penalty_factor)
            compute_order_amount.recent_lost_sales = 0
            compute_order_amount.recent_periods = 0
        else:
            dynamic_safety_adjustment = 1.0
    else:
        dynamic_safety_adjustment = 1.0
    
    # Calculate inventory target with dynamic safety stock
    inventory_target = base_stock_level + (safety_stock * dynamic_safety_adjustment)
    
    # Order amount based on inventory position and target
    raw_order_amount = max(0, inventory_target - current_position)
    
    # Maintain demand tracking for intelligent ordering
    if not hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'demand_estimate'):
        compute_order_amount.demand_estimate = 100.0
    
    # Update demand estimate using exponential smoothing
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'last_demand'):
        compute_order_amount.demand_estimate = (demand_smoothing_factor * compute_order_amount.last_demand + 
                                             (1 - demand_smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.demand_estimate)
    
    # Apply order smoothing to reduce volatility
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = (order_smoothing * compute_order_amount.prev_order + 
                        (1 - order_smoothing) * raw_order_amount)
        # Ensure order is reasonable relative to demand
        max_order = compute_order_amount.demand_estimate * 2.0
        smoothed_order = min(smoothed_order, max_order)
        order_amount = max(0, smoothed_order)
    else:
        order_amount = raw_order_amount
    
    # Store state for next period
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = order_amount
    compute_order_amount.last_on_hand = on_hand_inventory
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'last_demand'):
        compute_order_amount.last_demand = None  # Will be set externally
    
    return order_amount

POLICY #112
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Experiment: deepseek-chat_normal_std30_L4_c1_2_80_50_50_plain_processed_scipy_15_default_m2_r6
Generation: 4
Mechanism: function_attributes
Train Objective: 4114.78
Test Objective: 4164.7

Description: The original base stock policy with base_stock=300 was too conservative, resulting in high lost sales costs (90.36) compared to holding costs (11.68). I implemented a more sophisticated policy that: 1...

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Dynamic base stock level based on demand pattern recognition
    base_stock = 418.88188509560047  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 418.88188509560047, "min": 100, "max": 800, "type": "float"}
    
    safety_stock = 80.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 80.0, "min": 20, "max": 200, "type": "float"}
    smoothing_factor = 0.14868390297061856  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.14868390297061856, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.8, "type": "float"}
    
    lead_time_adj = 0.941780974975014  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.941780974975014, "min": 0.5, "max": 2.0, "type": "float"}
    
    pipeline_weight = 0.7  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.7, "min": 0.3, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    
    demand_boost = 1.0966995838332925  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.0966995838332925, "min": 1.0, "max": 1.5, "type": "float"}
    
    seasonal_factor = 1.0751008903105057  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.0751008903105057, "min": 0.8, "max": 1.4, "type": "float"}
    
    reorder_point = 119.99221849469605  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 119.99221849469605, "min": 50, "max": 300, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate effective pipeline (weighted sum with more weight on near-term orders)
    total_pipeline = sum(pipeline_orders)
    weighted_pipeline = sum(p * (pipeline_weight ** i) for i, p in enumerate(pipeline_orders))
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + total_pipeline
    
    # Adjust base stock based on lead time and demand variability
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock * lead_time_adj * demand_boost * seasonal_factor
    
    # Calculate target order quantity with smoothing
    target_order = max(0, adjusted_base_stock - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply smoothing to avoid drastic order changes
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = smoothing_factor * target_order + (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.prev_order
    else:
        smoothed_order = target_order
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = smoothed_order
    
    # Only order if below reorder point or significant gap exists
    if inventory_position < reorder_point or target_order > safety_stock:
        order_amount = max(0, round(smoothed_order))
    else:
        order_amount = 0
        
    return order_amount

    weighted_pipeline = sum(p * (pipeline_weight ** i) for i, p in enumerate(pipeline_orders))
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + total_pipeline
    
    # Adjust base stock based on lead time and demand variability
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock * lead_time_adj * demand_boost * seasonal_factor
    
    # Calculate target order quantity with smoothing
    target_order = max(0, adjusted_base_stock - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply smoothing to avoid drastic order changes
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = smoothing_factor * target_order + (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.prev_order
    else:
        smoothed_order = target_order
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = smoothed_order
    
    # Only order if below reorder point or significant gap exists
    if inventory_position < reorder_point or target_order > safety_stock:
        order_amount = max(0, round(smoothed_order))
    else:
        order_amount = 0
        
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Dynamic base stock level based on demand pattern recognition
    base_stock = 418.88188509560047  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 418.88188509560047, "min": 100, "max": 800, "type": "float"}
    
    safety_stock = 80.0  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 80.0, "min": 20, "max": 200, "type": "float"}
    smoothing_factor = 0.14868390297061856  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.14868390297061856, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.8, "type": "float"}
    
    lead_time_adj = 0.941780974975014  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.941780974975014, "min": 0.5, "max": 2.0, "type": "float"}
    
    pipeline_weight = 0.7  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.7, "min": 0.3, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
    
    demand_boost = 1.0966995838332925  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.0966995838332925, "min": 1.0, "max": 1.5, "type": "float"}
    
    seasonal_factor = 1.0751008903105057  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.0751008903105057, "min": 0.8, "max": 1.4, "type": "float"}
    
    reorder_point = 119.99221849469605  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 119.99221849469605, "min": 50, "max": 300, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate effective pipeline (weighted sum with more weight on near-term orders)
    total_pipeline = sum(pipeline_orders)
    weighted_pipeline = sum(p * (pipeline_weight ** i) for i, p in enumerate(pipeline_orders))
    
    # Calculate current inventory position
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + total_pipeline
    
    # Adjust base stock based on lead time and demand variability
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock * lead_time_adj * demand_boost * seasonal_factor
    
    # Calculate target order quantity with smoothing
    target_order = max(0, adjusted_base_stock - inventory_position)
    
    # Apply smoothing to avoid drastic order changes
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'prev_order'):
        smoothed_order = smoothing_factor * target_order + (1 - smoothing_factor) * compute_order_amount.prev_order
    else:
        smoothed_order = target_order
    compute_order_amount.prev_order = smoothed_order
    
    # Only order if below reorder point or significant gap exists
    if inventory_position < reorder_point or target_order > safety_stock:
        order_amount = max(0, round(smoothed_order))
    else:
        order_amount = 0
        
    return order_amount

Experiment: deepseek-chat_normal_std30_L4_c1_5_80_50_50_plain_no_scipy_15_default_m2_r2
Generation: 2
Mechanism: function_attributes

Test Objective: 6391.67043

Description: I redesigned the policy with several key improvements: 1) Added exponential smoothing for demand estimation using actual arriving orders rather than pipeline averages, providing more accurate forecast...

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Multi-level adaptive base stock policy with demand forecasting and seasonal adjustment
    base_stock_level = 362.2676239064787  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 362.2676239064787, "min": 200, "max": 600, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock_factor = 0.8868701880942833  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.8868701880942833, "min": 0.5, "max": 3.0, "type": "float"}
    demand_alpha = 0.20148188417863241  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.20148188417863241, "min": 0.05, "max": 0.3, "type": "float"}
    pipeline_adjustment = 0.9826379338614415  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.9826379338614415, "min": 0.3, "max": 1.2, "type": "float"}
    variability_buffer = 18.439756572565503  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 18.439756572565503, "min": 10, "max": 60, "type": "float"}
    lead_time_coverage = 1.402783633975658  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.402783633975658, "min": 1.0, "max": 3.0, "type": "float"}
    
    seasonal_factor = 0.8983521792289345  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.8983521792289345, "min": 0.8, "max": 1.3, "type": "float"}
    
    lost_sales_weight = 1.7833975805436544  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.7833975805436544, "min": 1.0, "max": 5.0, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate inventory position including pipeline
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Estimate demand using exponential smoothing from pipeline orders
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'demand_estimate'):
        recent_demand = pipeline_orders[0] if pipeline_orders else 100.0
        compute_order_amount.demand_estimate = (demand_alpha * recent_demand + 
                                            (1 - demand_alpha) * compute_order_amount.demand_estimate)
    else:
        compute_order_amount.demand_estimate = 100.0  # Initial estimate
    
    # Calculate dynamic safety stock based on demand variability and cost ratio
    base_safety_stock = safety_stock_factor * compute_order_amount.demand_estimate
    cost_adjusted_safety = base_safety_stock * lost_sales_weight / (lost_sales_weight + 1)
    
    # Calculate lead time demand coverage
    lead_time_demand = lead_time_coverage * compute_order_amount.demand_estimate
    
    # Calculate target inventory level with seasonal adjustment
    target_inventory = (base_stock_level + cost_adjusted_safety + variability_buffer + 
                      lead_time_demand) * seasonal_factor
    
    # Calculate order amount with pipeline adjustment
    pipeline_contribution = pipeline_adjustment * sum(pipeline_orders)
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - on_hand_inventory - pipeline_contribution)
    
    # Apply smoothing to avoid extreme order fluctuations
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'last_order'):
        order_smoothing = 0.3
        order_amount = (order_smoothing * compute_order_amount.last_order + 
                      (1 - order_smoothing) * order_amount)
    
    compute_order_amount.last_order = order_amount
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Multi-level adaptive base stock policy with demand forecasting and seasonal adjustment
    base_stock_level = 362.2676239064787  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 362.2676239064787, "min": 200, "max": 600, "type": "float"}
    safety_stock_factor = 0.8868701880942833  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.8868701880942833, "min": 0.5, "max": 3.0, "type": "float"}
    demand_alpha = 0.20148188417863241  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.20148188417863241, "min": 0.05, "max": 0.3, "type": "float"}
    pipeline_adjustment = 0.9826379338614415  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.9826379338614415, "min": 0.3, "max": 1.2, "type": "float"}
    variability_buffer = 18.439756572565503  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 18.439756572565503, "min": 10, "max": 60, "type": "float"}
    lead_time_coverage = 1.402783633975658  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.402783633975658, "min": 1.0, "max": 3.0, "type": "float"}
    
    seasonal_factor = 0.8983521792289345  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.8983521792289345, "min": 0.8, "max": 1.3, "type": "float"}
    
    lost_sales_weight = 1.7833975805436544  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.7833975805436544, "min": 1.0, "max": 5.0, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate inventory position including pipeline
    inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Estimate demand using exponential smoothing from pipeline orders
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'demand_estimate'):
        recent_demand = pipeline_orders[0] if pipeline_orders else 100.0
        compute_order_amount.demand_estimate = (demand_alpha * recent_demand + 
                                            (1 - demand_alpha) * compute_order_amount.demand_estimate)
    else:
        compute_order_amount.demand_estimate = 100.0  # Initial estimate
    
    # Calculate dynamic safety stock based on demand variability and cost ratio
    base_safety_stock = safety_stock_factor * compute_order_amount.demand_estimate
    cost_adjusted_safety = base_safety_stock * lost_sales_weight / (lost_sales_weight + 1)
    
    # Calculate lead time demand coverage
    lead_time_demand = lead_time_coverage * compute_order_amount.demand_estimate
    
    # Calculate target inventory level with seasonal adjustment
    target_inventory = (base_stock_level + cost_adjusted_safety + variability_buffer + 
                      lead_time_demand) * seasonal_factor
    
    # Calculate order amount with pipeline adjustment
    pipeline_contribution = pipeline_adjustment * sum(pipeline_orders)
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - on_hand_inventory - pipeline_contribution)
    
    # Apply smoothing to avoid extreme order fluctuations
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'last_order'):
        order_smoothing = 0.3
        order_amount = (order_smoothing * compute_order_amount.last_order + 
                      (1 - order_smoothing) * order_amount)
    
    compute_order_amount.last_order = order_amount
    
    return order_amount

POLICY #128
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Experiment: deepseek-chat_normal_std30_L6_c1_2_80_50_50_plain_no_scipy_15_default_m2_r5
Generation: 3
Mechanism: function_attributes
Train Objective: 6514.54113
Test Objective: 6513.66761

Description: The improved policy incorporates several key enhancements: 1) Dynamic base stock adjustment based on pipeline variability to account for demand uncertainty, 2) Pipeline forecasting that considers only...

CODE:

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Calculate net inventory position
    net_inventory = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Demand forecasting parameters based on pipeline length
    forecast_horizon = 3  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 3, "min": 1, "max": 6, "type": "int"}
    
    # Base stock level that adapts to pipeline status
    base_stock = 348.8836724453865  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 348.8836724453865, "min": 100, "max": 500, "type": "float"}
    
    # Safety stock multiplier based on pipeline variability
    safety_multiplier = 1.0759694038481278  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.0759694038481278, "min": 0.5, "max": 2.5, "type": "float"}
    
    # Pipeline adjustment factor
    pipeline_factor = 0.3  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.3, "min": 0.3, "max": 1.2, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate average pipeline demand
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        avg_pipeline = sum(pipeline_orders) / len(pipeline_orders)
    else:
        avg_pipeline = 100.0  # Default estimate
    
    # Adjust base stock based on pipeline characteristics
    pipeline_variability = max(0.1, sum((q - avg_pipeline) ** 2 for q in pipeline_orders) / max(1, len(pipeline_orders))) ** 0.5
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock + safety_multiplier * pipeline_variability
    
    # Calculate order amount with pipeline consideration
    pipeline_contribution = sum(pipeline_orders[:forecast_horizon]) * pipeline_factor
    
    target_inventory = adjusted_base_stock - pipeline_contribution
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - net_inventory)
    
    # Smooth ordering to avoid extreme fluctuations
    max_order_increase = 149.16077534461007  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 149.16077534461007, "min": 50, "max": 300, "type": "float"}
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'last_order'):
        order_amount = min(order_amount, compute_order_amount.last_order + max_order_increase)
    
    compute_order_amount.last_order = order_amount
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
    # Calculate net inventory position
    net_inventory = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
    
    # Demand forecasting parameters based on pipeline length
    forecast_horizon = 3  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 3, "min": 1, "max": 6, "type": "int"}
    
    # Base stock level that adapts to pipeline status
    base_stock = 348.8836724453865  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 348.8836724453865, "min": 100, "max": 500, "type": "float"}
    
    # Safety stock multiplier based on pipeline variability
    safety_multiplier = 1.0759694038481278  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.0759694038481278, "min": 0.5, "max": 2.5, "type": "float"}
    
    # Pipeline adjustment factor
    pipeline_factor = 0.3  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.3, "min": 0.3, "max": 1.2, "type": "float"}
    
    # Calculate average pipeline demand
    if len(pipeline_orders) > 0:
        avg_pipeline = sum(pipeline_orders) / len(pipeline_orders)
    else:
        avg_pipeline = 100.0  # Default estimate
    
    # Adjust base stock based on pipeline characteristics
    pipeline_variability = max(0.1, sum((q - avg_pipeline) ** 2 for q in pipeline_orders) / max(1, len(pipeline_orders))) ** 0.5
    adjusted_base_stock = base_stock + safety_multiplier * pipeline_variability
    
    # Calculate order amount with pipeline consideration
    pipeline_contribution = sum(pipeline_orders[:forecast_horizon]) * pipeline_factor
    
    target_inventory = adjusted_base_stock - pipeline_contribution
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - net_inventory)
    
    # Smooth ordering to avoid extreme fluctuations
    max_order_increase = 149.16077534461007  # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 149.16077534461007, "min": 50, "max": 300, "type": "float"}
    if hasattr(compute_order_amount, 'last_order'):
        order_amount = min(order_amount, compute_order_amount.last_order + max_order_increase)
    
    compute_order_amount.last_order = order_amount
    
    return order_amount
